Motivation: 为解决模型效率问题，建立垂直自适应层跳过的理论基础，验证其在保持性能的同时减少计算资源的可行性。

Method: 1. 理论推导：建立期望FLOPs闭式解；证明跳过层模型在函数空间中的严格包含性；揭示与混合专家架构的对偶性。2. 算法实现：VALSE利用轻量级难度估计器评估输入难度，通过逐层门控机制选择性跳过冗余中间层，实现按需激活深度。

Result: 1. 给出了任意每样本跳过策略的计算成本公式。2. 证明了跳过层模型能有意义地近似全层函数空间。3. 提出了VALSE原型，实现了非连续层跳过机制。

Conclusion: 垂直深度稀疏性是水平宽度稀疏性的正交补充；VALSE提供了一种有效的逐样本动态推理路径选择方案，初步原型验证了其可行性。

Abstract: This paper establishes a theoretical framework for vertical adaptive layer skipping, proving three foundational results: (i) an Expected FLOPs formula (theorem 2) giving a closed-form expression for the computational cost of arbitrary per-sample skip schedules as a function of layer-wise skip probabilities; (ii) function-space superset (theorem 10) and strict inclusion (theorem 11) theorems showing that skip-layer models are strictly contained in---yet meaningfully approximate---the full-layer function space, with an explicit separating example; and (iii) a structural duality between VALSE and Mixture-of-Experts architectures (proposition 6), positioning vertical depth-wise sparsity as the orthogonal counterpart to horizontal width-wise sparsity. Building on this theory, we propose VALSE (Vertical Adaptive Layer Skipping for Efficiency), a per-sample, non-contiguous layer skipping method: a lightweight difficulty estimator scores each input from the first few layers, and per-layer gates selectively skip redundant layers---including arbitrary middle layers while retaining deeper ones---so that only the necessary depth is activated for each input, whose feasibility is preliminarily assessed at prototype scale.

</details>


### [67] [BioStudyBench: Evaluating Agents on Post-Cutoff Biomedical Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07614)
*David Li,Shaamil Karim,Christian Gensbigler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BioStudyBench，这是一个用于评估AI智能体能否在没有预先知晓答案的情况下，仅通过检索和数据分析来复现生物医学研究发现的基准测试。结果显示，提供数据访问权限可显著提升模型性能，但开放权重模型仍落后于封闭权重模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI评估未能将“分析能力”与“先验知识/检索已知答案”有效区分开来，导致无法准确衡量智能体在未见过的长期复杂任务中的真实推理和分析能力。

Method: 构建了一个包含25个长周期分析任务的基准（BioStudyBench），这些任务源自2026年7月至9月发表的研究（晚于大多数模型的训练数据截止时间）。智能体需通过工具查找并下载公共数据、搜索文献并进行数据分析。通过对比有数据和无数据两种情况下的表现，来衡量获取外部信息带来的增益。

Result: 在有数据访问权限的情况下，8个被评估模型的平均通过率相比基线（无数据）提高了47个百分点。封闭式权重的最佳模型通过率为94.7%，而开放式权重的最佳模型为81.3%。

Conclusion: Access to data and tools significantly improves performance in complex biomedical analysis tasks. However, open-weight models still lag behind closed-weight models in this benchmark. The benchmark effectively isolates analytical ability from prior knowledge by using post-cutoff publications.

Abstract: We evaluate whether AI agents can match the reported findings of published biomedical studies using public data. Existing evaluations do not consistently separate analysis from prior knowledge or retrieval of the published answer. We introduce BioStudyBench, a benchmark of 25 long-horizon analysis tasks drawn from studies first published between July and September 2026, after the developer-reported knowledge cutoffs of the models we evaluate, semi-automatically filtered down from 404,019 PubMed records. In each task, the agent receives a neutral research question but no data files, so it must find and download the relevant public data, search the literature through tools that return only records dated before its cutoff, and report findings through data analysis. To measure gains over prior knowledge, we run every task both with and without access to data and tools. Across eight models, access to data and tools raises the pass rate by 47 percentage points on average over the no-data baseline. Open-weight models across sizes trail closed-weight models, with the best open-weight model passing 81.3% of tasks against 94.7% for the best closed-weight model.

</details>


### [68] [Explore, Then Commit: Measurement-Efficient Scientific Law Discovery with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07620)
*Kautik Mandve,Dileepa Fernando*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究评估了一种“探索-承诺”协议，用于利用大型语言模型（LLM）发现科学定律。该协议通过结构化探针、自动数值诊断等机制，显著减少了测量次数并提高了方程预测精度，特别是在配备解释器功能的配置中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学定律的发现过程涉及选择测量数据并将证据转化为统治方程。旨在提高这一过程的效率（减少测量需求）和准确性，同时评估LLM在这一流程中的实际应用潜力。

Method: 采用“探索-承诺”协议：1) LLM提出假设；2) 程序化规划器收集测量数据；3) 新提示词根据固定观测值合成最终定律。结合了结构化探针、自动数值诊断、受限测量批次及可选的解释器访问功能。在576次NewtonBench试验中，针对12个物理模块，使用GPT-4.1-mini和中等难度GPT-4.1进行8种配置的对比实验。

Result: 1. **测量效率显著提升**：启用解释器的规划器每轮试验的测量次数大幅降低（例如GPT-4.1从22.5降至8.6次）。2. **精度大幅提高**：平均幅度基于均方根对数误差从2.514降至0.202（GPT-4.1-mini），从0.626降至0.149（GPT-4.1）。3. **符号准确率不稳定**：跨模块的符号准确率增益不一致，随机采集在与分歧评分竞争时表现相当。4. **普遍节约测量**：所有模块均观察到测量节省，但归因于采集质量还是批次约束尚不明确。

Conclusion: 该完整协议被证明是一种极具前景的高测量效率配置。然而，其因果组成部分的有效性以及在无噪声直接方程任务之外的泛化能力仍有待进一步研究解决。

Abstract: Scientific law discovery requires selecting measurements and converting evidence into a governing equation. We evaluate an explore-then-commit protocol in which a large language model proposes hypotheses, a programmatic planner gathers measurements, and a fresh prompt synthesizes the final law from fixed observations. The protocol combines structured probes, automatic numerical diagnostics, restricted measurement batches, and optional interpreter access. Across 576 NewtonBench trials, we compare eight configurations on 12 physics modules using GPT-4.1-mini and a medium-difficulty GPT-4.1 replication. On medium tasks, interpreter-enabled planners use 8.6 versus 22.5 measurements per trial for GPT-4.1-mini and 8.9 versus 43.0 for GPT-4.1. Their mean magnitude-based root-mean-squared logarithmic error falls from 2.514 to 0.202 and from 0.626 to 0.149, respectively. An additional audit retains incomplete and invalid submissions in a coverage-sensitive analysis. Observed symbolic-accuracy gains are less consistent across modules, and random acquisition is competitive with disagreement scoring. Measurement savings occur in every module, but unequal batch constraints prevent attributing them solely to acquisition quality. These results support the complete protocol as a promising measurement-efficient configuration, while leaving its causal components and generalization beyond noiseless direct-equation tasks unresolved.

</details>


### [69] [Learning to Outgrow a Theory: Experimental Discovery Beyond the Initial Hypothesis Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07627)
*SiYuan Ma,Albert Gao,Chunzheng Zhu,Xin Yan,Wenlong Zhang,Wenxin Zhang,Luqi Gong,Tianlin Li,Qixin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种实验模型类修订方法，通过联合优化结构编辑和诊断实验来解决固定假设空间的失败模式。在400个环境中实现了89.5%的精确恢复率，显著优于基线，并验证了跨任务迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学发现系统通常在固定的假设空间内优化实验。当所有候选项都遗漏相同的潜在机制时，会导致模型类系统性错误，此时即便候选项之间出现分歧，仍无法找到正确答案。需要一种能自动修正模型类别的方法。

Method: 构建了实验模型类修订框架， discovery policy 联合提出结构性编辑（structural edit）和诊断实验。耦合了两个关键要素：1）类级可区分性目标，要求单一参数化解释所有选中实验；2）随时有效的序贯证据（anytime-valid sequential evidence），仅在当前模型类被证伪后触发结构性修订。

Result: 在400个控制动力学环境中，该联合策略在使用32次真实实验预算下达到了89.5%的精确恢复率，比最强基线提高10.0个百分点，且所需执行实验数和候选拟合数更少。该方法在未见过的机制组合、参数外推及成本变化中具有强泛化能力。当真实机制超出编辑语法库时，能以88%的检出率和5.5%的错误支持率检测库的不足。结果在ODEBench、ODEBase和DiscoverPhysics等基准中均有效。

Conclusion: 科学发现应将“理论应表达哪些机制”与“在哪里收集证据”视为一个单一的序贯决策问题进行统一处理，而非分离的两个步骤。

Abstract: Scientific discovery systems typically optimize experiments within a fixed hypothesis space. This creates a failure mode when all available candidates omit the same missing mechanism: candidate disagreement can collapse even while the model class is systematically wrong. We formulate experimental model-class revision, in which a discovery policy jointly proposes a structural edit and a diagnostic experiment that tests whether that edit is necessary. The method couples a class-level distinguishability objective, in which one shared parameterization must explain all selected experiments, with anytime-valid sequential evidence that triggers structural revision only after the current class is rejected. On 400 held-out controlled dynamical environments, the joint policy reaches 89.5% exact recovery with a budget of 32 real experiments, improving the strongest matched baseline by 10.0 percentage points while requiring fewer executed experiments and candidate fits. The learned revision-experiment pairing transfers across unseen mechanism combinations, held-out but expressible primitives, parameter extrapolation, and shifted experiment costs; when the true mechanism is outside the edit grammar, it detects library insufficiency in 88% of cases with a 5.5% false-support rate. Revision gains also transfer to ODEBench and ODEBase model-library tasks, as well as DiscoverPhysics worlds. These results support a view of scientific discovery in which deciding what mechanisms a theory should make expressible and where to collect evidence are treated as a single sequential decision problem.

</details>


### [70] [Measuring climate backlash in Twitter and Reddit archives: Lexical definitions, recorded responses and participant turnover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07634)
*Wentao Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用透明、非排他性的词汇规则处理完整的Twitter和Reddit档案数据，旨在揭示社交媒体中气候行动抵制的话语测量问题。研究发现平台特性、参与者流动及术语定义显著影响对“气候倒退”的量化结果，强调了操作化定义对研究结论的关键约束作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的社交媒体档案常用于研究对气候行动的抵制，但仅凭词汇、回复计数和观测到的参与者无法准确衡量同一社会过程。需要更严谨的方法论来厘清这些指标之间的差异及其对研究结论的影响。

Method: 作者分析了四个提供的Twitter和Reddit档案，采用全样本处理而非抽样方法，应用透明且非排他的词汇规则。研究将框架共现与源特定的时间和响应模型联系起来，并区分了回归作者的事件期间变化与参与者更替的影响。

Result: 1. Reddit上可再生能源术语与成本相关语言并存，而狭窄的反拨短语削弱了跨源对比并逆转了《巴黎协定》提交的正负号对比。
2. 跨话语历史未能提高主要语境预测的可信度。
3. Twitter上的否定/骗局术语与更高的点赞数相关，而Reddit的响应关联取决于框架、结果和作者规格。
4. 在2019年全球气候罢工期间，回归作者的表达和参与者更替共同增加了抗议语言的份额。
5. 档案端点阻止了相应的Twitter迁移推论；大多数跨簇估计缺乏置信区间，限制了正式推断。

Conclusion: 操作化定义、平台特定的响应字段以及观测边界决定了关于气候反拨（backlash）可以得出的结论。本研究的贡献在于提供了关于这些测量后果的基于档案的解释，而非直接衡量个体反对、说服或倡导导致的反拨。

Abstract: Social media archives are often used to study resistance to climate action, but words, response counters and observed participants do not measure the same social process. We examine four supplied Twitter and Reddit archives by processing all registered files without sampling and applying transparent, non-exclusive lexical rules. The study links frame co-occurrence to source-specific temporal and response models, then separates event-period changes among returning authors from participant turnover. Renewable-energy terms accompany cost-related language on Reddit, yet narrower backlash phrases sharply reduce cross-source contrasts and reverse the sign of the Paris Agreement contrast in submissions. Cross-discourse history does not improve eligible primary-context forecasts. Denial/hoax terms are associated with higher recorded Twitter likes, whereas Reddit response associations depend on frame, outcome and author specification. Around the 2019 global climate strike, returning-author expression and participant turnover both contribute to increased protest-language shares. An archive endpoint prevents the corresponding Climate Twitter migration inference. Most crossed-cluster estimates lack released intervals, and joint author/month response covariance estimates fail, restricting formal inference. These results show how operational definitions, platform-specific response fields and observation boundaries shape what can be claimed about climate backlash. The contribution is an archive-based account of these measurement consequences, rather than a measure of individual opposition, persuasion or advocacy-induced backlash.

</details>


### [71] [Learning Explainable Representations of Complex Game-playing Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07638)
*Abhijeet Krishnan,Colin M. Potts,Arnav Jhala,Harshad Khadilkar,Shirish Karande,Chris Martens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模拟人类认知过程的强化学习（RL）训练方法，使智能体能将游戏策略自动编译为可执行程序。通过在棋类和网格环境中验证，证明了该方法能从历史数据中高效学习策略并产生有效行动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿人类玩家通过抽象概念和策略来提升复杂游戏表现的能力，解决传统RL在理解对手策略及自身行动决策上耗时费力且缺乏可解释性的问题。

Method: 设计一种类认知的训练机制，将学习到的策略和政策转化为基于游戏操作序列的可执行程序（Programs）。通过算法自动学习这些程序以应用于国际象棋和网格环境任务求解。

Result: 成功开发出能自动学习程序的算法，在国际象棋和网格环境中实现了策略的自动化构建。实验表明，所学成的策略能够生成有效的行动，并且完全可以从现有的游戏对局数据中学习获得。

Conclusion: 将人类抽象思维引入RL训练是可行的，能有效提升智能体的策略合成能力与学习效率，为游戏AI及复杂任务规划提供了新的可解释性解决方案。

Abstract: As part of learning to play complex games, human players develop develop abstractions for concepts and strategies of gameplay consistent with game rules to improve their performance. These concepts are applied to explain other players' actions, and to inform their own actions in-game. Understanding other players' strategies is a crucial part of such improvement, but requires time and effort. In this paper, we propose a strategy similar to human cognition for training RL agents to synthesize learned strategies and policies as executable procedures based on sequences of gameplay actions. We present methods to automatically learn such programs to play chess and to solve tasks in a grid-based environment. We show that the learned strategies produce effective actions, and can be learned from gameplay data.

</details>


### [72] [Towards the Automatic Synthesis of Interpretable Chess Tactics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07640)
*Abhijeet Krishnan,Chris Martens*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于归纳逻辑编程的符号子策略模型用于下棋，旨在通过融入领域知识提高AI策略的可解释性，并验证其在下棋走法建议上与人类初级玩家水平的相似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习智能体虽在围棋、星际争霸等游戏中超越人类专家，但其策略缺乏可解释性。解读这些策略有助于人类玩家提升技艺，因此需要一种能结合领域知识以提高可解释性的方法。

Method: 受国际象棋战术启发，利用名为PAL的归纳逻辑编程系统习得的模式来构建一个符号子策略模型，以整合领域知识并增强模型的可解释性。

Result: 开发了一种发散度指标以评估模型相对于随机基线的表现，发现该模型能够建议与人类初级玩家水平相当的走法。

Conclusion: 通过将模型集成到现成的引擎中，提出了该模型的计算评估方案，初步工作证明了符号化方法在国际象棋策略解释方面的可行性。

Abstract: State-of-the-art reinforcement learning agents are capable of outperforming human experts at games like chess, Go and StarCraft II. These agents do not simply take advantage of their digital hardware in being able to react and calculate faster than humans, but employ better strategies that lead to more victories. Interpreting these strategies would give human players valuable insight into how to improve their play. In this preliminary work, we propose a symbolic sub-policy model for playing chess. Inspired by chess tactics, our model attempts to incorporate domain knowledge to improve interpretability. We adapt patterns learned by an inductive logic programming system called PAL to derive our model. We contribute a divergence metric to evaluate our model against a random baseline, and find a set of tactics that is able to suggest moves of similar playing strength to a human beginner. Finally, we propose a computational evaluation scheme for the model by augmenting an off-the-shelf engine with it.

</details>


### [73] [Matching Object or Relation? Tracing Abstract Reasoning Inside VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07646)
*Gouki Minegishi,Hiroki Furuta,Takeshi Kojima,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过结合比较心理学中的关系匹配到样本（RMTS）范式与模型内部机制分析，揭示了视觉语言模型（VLMs）在抽象推理任务中失败的原因。研究发现VLM的抽象推理由两个竞争性回路支撑：早期表层特征回路与晚期抽象关系回路，并识别出提升性能的四个关键因素，为理解VLM如何实现抽象推理提供了机制层面的见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试记录了VLM在需要抽象推理的任务中表现不佳，但无法解释“为什么”会发生这种情况或缺失了哪种认知能力。本研究旨在填补这一空白，深入探究VLM抽象推理能力的内在机制及局限。

Method: 采用比较和发展心理学中的关系匹配到样本（RMTS）范式，结合对模型内部的机制分析。评估对象包括前沿API模型（GPT, Claude, Gemini）和三个开源家族（Qwen3.5, Gemma-4, InternVL3）。具体方法包括参数控制的刺激集评估、每层表示相似性分析以及因果中介分析，并在ARC-AGI-1数据集上进行了扩展验证。

Result: 识别出四个影响VLM向关系匹配能力转变的关键杠杆：能力层级、模型规模、每场景物体数量以及无逐物体噪声。这些杠杆共同产生了一种类似于人类“关系转变”的发展轨迹。机制分析显示，VLM抽象推理由两个竞争性回路实现：早期回路基于表面物体特征组织图像，晚期回路基于抽象关系组织图像。消融实验表明，针对RMTS识别出的关系头部进行消融比随机消融性能下降更显著，证实该回路被用于更广泛的推理任务。

Conclusion: 通过机制级别的视角，本研究阐明了VLM中抽象推理的实现方式及其局限性。发现早期和晚期回路的竞争是理解VLM认知能力的关键，这种机制层面的理解有助于推动未来VLM在复杂抽象推理任务上的发展。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) excel on visual benchmarks but fail systematically on tasks requiring abstract reasoning. Existing benchmarks document this failure but cannot say \emph{why} it happens or which cognitive capability is missing. We close this gap by adopting the Relational Match-to-Sample (RMTS) paradigm from comparative and developmental psychology and pairing it with a mechanistic analysis of the model's internals. On a parametrically controlled stimulus set evaluated across frontier API models (GPT, Claude, Gemini) and three open-source families (Qwen3.5, Gemma-4, InternVL3), we identify four levers that shift VLMs toward the relational match---capability tier, model scale, the number of objects per scene, and the absence of per-object stimulus noise---together producing a developmental-like trajectory that mirrors the human \emph{relational shift}. Opening up the model, a per-layer representational similarity analysis and a causal mediation analysis reveal that VLM abstract reasoning is implemented by two competing circuits: an early circuit that organises images by their surface object features, and a late circuit that organises them by their abstract relation. Extending the analysis to ARC-AGI-1, we find that ablating the relational heads identified on RMTS degrades performance more than ablating random heads, indicating that the relational circuit is recruited beyond our controlled stimuli. We hope this mechanism-level view serves as a step toward understanding how abstract reasoning is implemented in VLMs.

</details>


### [74] [Where Rules End and Judges Begin: Measuring the Judgment Boundary in Multi-Agent Systems Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07657)
*Shaswata Mitra,Raj Patel,Subash Neupane,Sudip Mittal,Md Rayhanur Rahman,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对LLM多智能体系统（MAS）防御评估孤立且昂贵的问题，本研究提出DEFER1框架，结合28项确定性检查与4名“法官”组成的残差判断机制。该方法将攻击成功率从30%降至3%，其中78%的攻击由规则阻断，其余交由法官处理，有效解决了策略违规与意图掩饰两类威胁的平衡难题，但也暴露了风险评分门控准确性的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAS防御通常孤立评估单一攻击类型，导致后果成本高且难以审计；面对多智能体系统中常见的对抗性内容，缺乏能够同时高效处理明确政策违规与隐蔽意图欺骗的综合防御机制。

Method: 提出DEFER1（基于确定性优先执行的残差判断）防御框架，其核心包括级联的28项确定性检查规则，用于拦截明确违规内容；对于未命中规则的剩余提案，将其移交由四名“法官”组成的裁决小组进行综合判断。

Result: 在四个独立领域的测试中，攻击成功率从约30.0%显著降低至约3.0%；78%的被阻断攻击由确定性检查处理；在安全运营领域，仅有四分之一的提案进入法官裁决环节，表明规则能有效覆盖清晰的政策违规，而法官主要处理意图模糊或掩饰的案例。

Conclusion: 分层防御策略（规则+人工/智能裁决）能显著提升MAS的安全性，但同时也揭示了当前威胁评估中的局限性，如风险评分门控存在误报率高、对合法提案批准率低等问题，凸显了精准量化威胁难度的挑战。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) engage tools, share memory, and delegate tasks, often encountering adversarial content. Current defenses for MAS are typically evaluated in isolation, focusing on one attack type at a time, which can lead to costly and hard-to-audit outcomes. This study organizes defenses into five principles, implementing them as DEFER1 (DEterministic-First Enforcement with Residual judgment), which includes a cascade of 28 checks that blocks what it can and refers the rest to a panel of four judges. In independent testing across four domains, attack success rates drop from about 30.0% to approximately 3.0%, with 78% of blocked attacks handled by deterministic checks. Only a quarter of proposals reach the judges in the security-operations domain, illustrating that the rules provide security for attacks violating clear policies, while judges manage those that only misrepresent intent. Both systems have weaknesses, such as a risk-score approval gate that inaccurately approves most attack proposals but few legitimate ones, highlighting the challenges in assessing threats accurately.

</details>


### [75] [Massive Activation Gating Channel in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07661)
*Minjia Mao,Shi Chen,Bowen Yin,Xiao Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大语言模型中“大规模激活”现象的根本机制，指出该现象由输入嵌入层到脉冲前馈网络（FFN）的一个特定通道——大规模激活门控通道（MAGC）控制。当MAGC值超过阈值时，FFN输出渐近简化为二次型，从而引发大规模激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大规模激活在大型语言模型中普遍存在，但其通过预训练LLM传播时的形成机制尚不清楚，缺乏深入的理论解释。

Method: 通过分析六个跨越四种模型家族和不同规模的LLM，定位并验证了单一控制通道的存在；结合理论推导，分析了MAGC值极大或极小时FFN输出的渐近行为及其与权重矩阵列的结构关系。

Result: 发现了一个位置固定于特定LLM中的“大规模激活门控通道”（MAGC）。实验验证了该通道在不同模型中的存在性及效应；理论证明当MAGC值足够大时，FFN输出退化为混合少量下投影矩阵列的二次型，这些列具有大规模激活的形状特征。

Conclusion: 大规模激活是由特定的MAGC通道控制的确定性现象。理解MAGC及其引发的二次型渐近行为，有助于深入解析LLM内部信息处理的动态机制。

Abstract: Massive activations, a phenomenon in which a small number of hidden channels exhibit exceptionally large magnitudes, are pervasive in large language models (LLMs). However, the mechanism by which a token develops massive activations as it propagates through a pretrained LLM remains poorly understood. In this paper, we find that the emergence of massive activations is controlled by a single channel in the input embedding to a spike feed-forward network (FFN). The position of this channel is fixed for a particular LLM. We name this channel the massive activation gating channel (MAGC). When the value of the MAGC is sufficiently large (or small, depending on the LLM), the output of the spike FFN exhibits massive activations. Examining six LLMs across four model families and different model sizes, we verify the existence and effect of MAGC. We further provide a theoretical explanation of the mechanism by which MAGC induces massive activations. When the value of MAGC is sufficiently large (or small), the output of a spike FFN asymptotically reduces to a quadratic form that mixes a few columns of the down-projection matrix of the FFN. Since these columns exhibit the shape of massive activations, the output therefore exhibits massive activations.

</details>


### [76] [EIO-Agents: The Missing Semantic Layer for AI Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07675)
*Fouad Bousetouane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对AI Agent评估缺乏统一语义标准的问题，本文提出EIO-Agents规范，通过EIO提供语义层（定义证据与决策关系）和PER提供记录层（确保可追溯与验证），实现从分数到可审计的 accountable artifact 的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI Agent在生产环境中日益重要，但评估结果（如分数、轨迹、判决输出）缺乏共享的语义标准，往往无法明确证据支持的主张、证据的证明力及主张如何导向最终决定，导致评估缺乏透明度和可解释性。

Method: 引入EIO-Agents开放规范，包含两个核心层级：1. Evaluation Intelligence Ontology (EIO)：提供语义层，通过类型化证据、版本化的行为谓词、证据契约、声明、见证规则、证明状态等构建可计算的推导逻辑；2. Portable Evaluation Record (PER)：作为系统记录，提供内容寻址的标准化表示，保留从证据到决策的完整链条。

Result: EIO解决了传统评估中证据含义不明的问题，提供了缺失的语义契约；PER确保了评估结果的可移植性、可重新推导、可解释和可验证性。该框架将评估从简单的分数集合转化为意义明确、证据充分、局限可查的负责任产物。

Conclusion: 随着AI Agent承担更多操作责任，评估必须超越单纯的分数和裁决，转变为具有独立性检查能力的负责性产物（accountable artifact）。EIO-Agents通过标准化语义和记录机制，确立了更严谨、透明的AI Agent评估范式。

Abstract: AI agents are entering production in increasingly consequential environments without a shared semantic standard for what their evaluations actually mean. Scores, traces, judge outputs, and multi juror findings are increasingly used to justify readiness and release decisions, yet they often do not specify what evidence supports a claim, what that evidence can establish, or how the claim leads to a decision. We introduce EIO-Agents, an open specification for interoperable AI agent evaluation built on two layers. The Evaluation Intelligence Ontology (EIO) provides the semantic layer through typed evidence, versioned behavioral predicates, evidence contracts, claims, witness rules, proof status, recurrence, and computable derivations for metrics, findings, controls, and PASS, REVIEW, or BLOCK decisions. The Portable Evaluation Record (PER) provides the system of record: a canonical, content addressed representation of one evaluation that preserves the evidence to decision chain and can be re derived, explained, and verified. Scores summarize, juries interpret, and traces record, but none of them define what the evidence means or what it can prove. EIO provides that missing semantic contract, while PER preserves the resulting evaluation as a portable and verifiable system of record. As AI agents assume greater operational responsibility, evaluation must become more than a collection of scores and verdicts; it must become an accountable artifact whose meaning, evidence, limitations, and decisions can be independently checked.

</details>


### [77] [BluffJAX: Adversarial Imperfect Information Games in JAX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07686)
*Aryaman Reddi,Jan Peters,Carlo D'Eramo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BluffJAX是一个基于JAX的开源工具包，专为不完美的信息对抗游戏设计，支持GPU加速的高吞吐量仿真。它涵盖了德州扑克、库恩扑克等经典游戏及 bluff、stud 等新领域游戏，旨在通过高性能基准测试和RL算法基线来推动博弈论在强化学习中的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习研究中缺乏对多样化博弈机制和高效率并行计算的支持，难以充分利用GPU加速优势进行大规模仿真和算法比较。

Method: 构建一个包含多种经典与新晋游戏的 JAX 环境套件；针对单/多 GPU场景优化内存与吞吐量性能；提供强化学习、树搜索和博弈求解算法的标准化基准测试结果。

Result: 实现了高达每秒数亿样本的处理速度，证明了其在单卡和双卡设置下的可扩展性；相比现有的CPU和相关GPU库具有显著性能优势；提供了全面的算法基线数据。

Conclusion: BluffJAX 能有效促进基于博弈论的强化学习方法的研究，其高性能和多游戏支持为未来创新提供了新的挑战和评估平台。

Abstract: We introduce BluffJAX: an open-source suite of adversarial imperfect information games in JAX. We provide canonical implementations of games designed for high simulation throughputs and parallelization on GPU accelerators. Our suite consists of well-studied benchmarks such as Texas Hold'Em Poker and Kuhn Poker, as well as games that have not been previously studied in reinforcement learning research, such as Bluff, Stud Poker, and Kemps. We hope that implementing a variety of game mechanics and difficulties will introduce new challenges and foster novel research directions in game-theoretic methods for RL. We benchmark the throughput performance and memory usage of our environments in single and multi-GPU settings, demonstrating scaling of up to hundreds of millions of samples per second, and motivating the usage of BluffJAX over related GPU and CPU-based libraries. We benchmark reinforcement learning, tree search, and game-solving algorithms in JAX in order to provide users with baseline results and facilitate future comparisons.

</details>


### [78] [On the Boundary of Admission Gates: An Injected-Truth Study of Falsification-First Selection in Quantitative Strategy Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07701)
*Tianlun Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出策略研究中的“采纳门槛”统计检验在弱信号下虽能消除虚假发现，但会严重限制采纳率（1-7%），且在强信号下无效；更严重的是，基于绝对收益而非超额收益计算的筛选标准会错误地拒绝所有候选策略，包括真实信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决策略研究中混淆两个问题：寻找盈利规则与验证发现非搜索运气。现有缺乏对统计采纳门槛（admission gates）有效性及其成本的实际测试，需建立可靠的方法论以区分真信号与假阳性。

Method: 引入“注入真理协议”（injected-truth protocol）结合随机控制组，通过对比统计门槛是否优于随机基准来验证其信息价值。使用合成数据和校准后的真实数据进行实证分析，并比较基于绝对收益与超额收益的不同筛选标准。

Result: 1. 在弱信号 regimes 下，统计门槛能有效消除虚假发现，但导致采纳率急剧下降至1-7%；2. 在强信号 regimes 下，门槛不增加任何信息价值；3. 基于绝对收益计算的筛选标准具有破坏性，会无声地拒绝所有候选项，连真实信号也无法幸免。

Conclusion: 统计采纳门槛并非万能药，其应用存在显著代价和信息局限。研究者应警惕使用绝对收益指标进行筛选导致的系统性误杀，需谨慎评估多重测试背景下的策略验证方法的有效性。

Abstract: Strategy research conflates two problems: finding a profitable rule, and establishing that the finding is not search luck. The latter calls for admission gates -- statistical criteria that must be satisfied before a conclusion is adopted -- yet whether gates work, and at what cost, remains untested. We introduce an injected-truth protocol with a random-admission control that adopts at the same rate as the gate; only if the gate beats this control does it carry information rather than merely raise a threshold. Across synthetic and real-calibrated panels, gates eliminate false discoveries in the weak-signal regime but cut adoption to 1--7%, and add nothing when signals are strong. Most importantly, criteria computed on absolute rather than excess returns silently reject every candidate, including true signals. Keywords: multiple testing, backtest overfitting, strategy admission, injected-truth validation, excess returns, false discovery rate

</details>


### [79] [AgentMemGate: Addressing Speculation Contamination in Conversational Assistant Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07707)
*Chirag Sharma,Benjamin Fowlersmith,Karime Maamari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AgentMemGate解决对话AI中长期记忆里的“猜测污染”问题，通过写入时门控过滤未完成的计划，显著提升记忆准确性和任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话AI助手在提取用户事实进入长期记忆时，会将“陈述的计划/猜测”错误地记录为既定事实（如将“可能搬去西雅图”记为已居住）。这种“猜测污染”导致记忆失真，而现有的最终状态基准测试因缺乏对中间状态的探测和未解决猜测的样本，无法发现此错误。

Method: 1. 构建AgentMemGate：一种用于配置文件存储的写入时门控机制，利用分类器将提取的语句分为“猜测、已完成事件、修正或其他”。
2. 策略执行：推测性内容不直接进入记忆，而是保留并设定后续晋升或删除的条件；仅当确认为事实后才存入。
3. 数据集贡献：提供包含多会话对话的数据集，涵盖计划被确认、放弃或未解决的情况。
4. 评估基准：在147个对话的保留集及核心基准上进行测试对比。

Result: 1. 污染消除：在核心基准上，相较于无门控管道，AgentMemGate将最易受污染的提取风格的观察到的污染从87.5%降至0%。
2. 精度提升：核心基准上的任务准确率从65%提升至95%；在更难的保留集上，准确率提升9-13个百分点，残留污染率仅为3.4%-5.7%。
3. 基线对比：Mem0和Graphiti在未解决计划的误报率分别为35.2%和27.3%，凸显了新方法的有效性。
4. 瓶颈识别：主要剩余瓶颈是字段匹配问题，即真实场景中的猜测常因无法匹配任何配置文件字段而无法进入门控处理流程。

Conclusion: AgentMemGate有效解决了长期记忆中的猜测污染问题，显著提高了系统准确性。尽管消除了大部分由显式猜测引起的错误，但字段匹配仍是实现全面鲁棒性的主要障碍。研究开源了数据集、提示词和评估代码。

Abstract: Conversational AI assistants with long-term memory extract facts from user messages into a store consulted in later conversations. A stated plan can enter that store as fact: a user who might move to Seattle may be recorded as already living there. We call this speculation contamination. Final-state memory benchmarks miss this error because they do not probe intermediate state and include few unresolved speculations. We present AgentMemGate, a write-time gate for profile-store memory that classifies extracted statements as speculation, completed event, correction, or other. Speculations remain outside memory, with conditions governing later promotion or deletion. We also contribute a dataset of multi-session conversations in which plans are confirmed, abandoned, or left unresolved. On our 147-conversation held-out set, Mem0 and Graphiti assert unresolved plans as current state for 35.2% and 27.3% of pending plans. On the core benchmark, AgentMemGate eliminates all observed contamination relative to the identical ungated pipeline (87.5% to zero for the most exposed extraction style) and raises task accuracy from 65% to 95%. On the harder held-out set, gated contamination is 3.4% to 5.7% and task accuracy rises by 9 to 13 percentage points. Our analysis identifies field matching as the main remaining bottleneck: realistic speculations often match no profile field and never reach the gate. We release our datasets, prompts, and evaluation code.

</details>


### [80] [Evidence Before Sampling: Interpretable Implicit Negative Candidate Discovery for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07708)
*Shreya Rajpal,Sonia Sharma,Swapnil Parekh,Lisa Li,Jeyendran Balakrishnan,Nagaraj Janardhana,Andrew Mattarella-Micke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于符号规则和LLM的隐式负样本发现方法，通过统计证据和业务目标对齐来筛选高质量的未观察交互作为负反馈，显著提升了工业界B2B场景下的推荐模型性能及可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习推荐系统依赖负采样处理缺失的显式负反馈，但简单的“未观察即负面”假设缺乏解释性，且未能区分用户真正不感兴趣与证据不足的情况。特别是在商业场景中，需要可解释、符合业务目标的负信号，以应对稀疏和偏斜的实际数据问题。

Method: 1. 模式编码：将观测到的客户行为转化为表示未观察交互的符号规则（Symbolic Rules）。
2. 评分与排序：基于支持度（support）、信息量（informativeness）和产品相关性对产品相关性强度的规则进行评分，并根据证据强度排序。
3. LLM解读：利用大语言模型结合业务目标和领域知识解释保留后的规则。
4. 报告生成：将LLM的业务解读与统计证据结合，形成最终的负候选评估报告。

Result: 在工业B2B设置及五个公开数据集上进行了评估。结果显示：
1. 候选质量评估中，该方法在三个公开数据集和工业场景中表现出比基线更高的精度。
2. 在工业任务中，相比每正例对应四个负例的随机选择，使用符号选择将下游测试PR-AUC提升了12.5%。
3. 证明了负候选的有效性可以独立于下游推荐性能进行评估。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了基于证据、业务对齐且可解释的负样本选择。它解决了传统负采样的模糊性问题，通过分离负候选验证与下游训练，改善了稀疏、偏斜现实世界推荐系统中的模型训练效果和可解释性。

Abstract: Recommender systems learn from observed user-item interactions, but explicit negative feedback is often unavailable. Since deep learning models require negative signals for training, negative sampling methods typically treat selected unobserved interactions as negatives. However, a missing interaction does not explain why a user is uninterested in an item or whether there is sufficient evidence to label it negative. This is especially important in business recommendation, where negative signals should be interpretable and aligned with business objectives. We formulate implicit negative candidate discovery to identify unobserved interactions supported by observed customer behavior. We encode these patterns as symbolic rules, score them based on support, informativeness, and product relevance, and rank the retained rules by evidence. An LLM then interprets the retained rules using business objectives and domain knowledge; the interpretations are combined with the statistical evidence in the final report. We evaluate our method in an industrial B2B setting and across five public recommendation datasets. Candidate-quality evaluations in the industrial setting and three public datasets show higher precision than the evaluated baselines, while symbolic selection improves downstream test PR-AUC by 12.5% over random selection with four negatives per positive example in the industrial task. Our results show that negative candidate validity can be evaluated separately from downstream recommendation performance. This distinction enables evidence-based, business-aligned, and explainable negative selection, improving both interpretability and model training in sparse, skewed, real-world recommendation settings.

</details>


### [81] [PERSIST: Who-What-When Memory Across Sessions for Full-Duplex Spoken Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07725)
*Achira Lin,Siyuan Hou,Wenyi Yu,Xinnian Zhao,Haoyu Niu,Wang Geng,Longshuai Xiao,Shihai Xiao,Mangsuo Zhao,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PERSIST系统，通过Who-What-When联合评分机制解决多用户对话中的记忆检索问题；引入SpokenTrace基准测试；将检索延迟从578.42ms降至7.03ms，端到端准确率达85.08%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语音助手需处理多用户共享场景，要求系统能根据过往交互个性化行为或回答特定问题（如识别说话人、恢复相关历史状态、区分后续修订），而非仅基于话题相似性检索片段。

Method: 构建跨会话可读事件记录以明确建模“谁(Who)、什么(What)、何时(When)”；采用结合语义内容、声纹身份和时间状态的3W联合评分机制进行检索；利用对话骨干网络的中间表示复用，避免查询音频重编码以实现实时全双工交互。

Result: 在提出的SpokenTrace基准上，PERSIST实现85.08%的端到端任务准确率；将所有支持项的EM@3指标从BGE-large的49.01%提升至82.10%；检索延迟大幅降低至7.03 ms。

Conclusion: PERSIST通过显式建模多维身份与时间状态，有效解决了多会话多人对话中的精准记忆检索问题，显著提升了召回率、说话人归因及时间状态追踪能力，并实现了低延迟实时交互。

Abstract: Modern voice assistants may be shared by multiple users and should be able to answer questions about earlier conversations such as "When did I originally plan to leave?" or adapt their behavior to individual users based on past interactions. This requires more than retrieving a topically similar passage: the assistant must identify the current speaker, recover the relevant past state, and distinguish it from later revisions. We present PERSIST, a persistent memory system for multi-session, multi-speaker spoken dialogue that explicitly models Who, What, and When. PERSIST structures cross-session histories into readable event records and retrieves them with a 3W joint scoring mechanism that combines semantic content, acoustic speaker identity, and temporal state. For real-time full-duplex interaction, PERSIST further reuses intermediate representations from the dialogue backbone, avoiding query-audio re-encoding and reducing retrieval latency from 578.42 ms to 7.03 ms. We also introduce SpokenTrace, a diagnostic benchmark that factorizes evaluation along memory tasks and speaker-query types, exposing failures in recall, speaker attribution, and temporal-state tracking. On SpokenTrace, PERSIST achieves 85.08% end-to-end task accuracy and improves all-support EM@3 from 49.01% with BGE-large to 82.10%.

</details>


### [82] [How Well Do LLMs Reason with Noisy Evidence? An Active Visual Reasoning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07751)
*Bach Nguyen,Zhaonan Li,Mau Son Nguyen,Sanika Chavan,Nilay Kumar,Hong Anh Nguyen,Khoa Vo,Ben Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VisualNoiseQA基准，测试LLM在噪声视觉反馈下的主动推理能力。通过利用现有VLM作为随机传感器并暴露不确定性信号（基于自一致性），评估模型如何收集信息、决定查询策略及终止条件，以解决不确定环境下的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的推理并非静态问答，代理需从经常不可靠的工具备份中主动获取信息。现有基准通常假设反馈可信或未提供明确的校准不确定性信号，导致缺乏对LLM在证据本身具有不确定性时如何进行推理的研究。

Method: 构建了一个新的基准VisualNoiseQA：让纯文本LLM通过将固定的现成VLM视为随机视觉传感器来迭代查询以解决VQA问题。通过对每次查询进行多次采样，利用自一致性暴露经验不确定性信号。筛选出传感器不一致但人类可解决的多样化问题实例，确保场景的自然性和挑战性。

Result: 在涵盖感知、图表理解和知识密集型推理的1,000个实例上评估了多个LLM推理器。结果表明该基准提供了一个受控平台，能够研究不同LLM如何利用不确定性信号实现鲁棒推理，揭示了模型在处理噪声和不确定性时的行为差异。

Conclusion: VisualNoiseQA填补了主动推理领域中针对噪声视觉反馈和不确定性处理的空白，为评估和改进LLM在动态、不可靠环境中的决策能力和鲁棒性提供了重要基准。

Abstract: Real-world reasoning rarely reduces to static question answering: agents must actively gather information from tools and sensors that are often noisy and unreliable. Yet most existing active reasoning benchmarks assume that environmental feedback is trustworthy, or introduce noise without exposing an explicit, calibrated uncertainty signal, leaving open how LLMs should reason when the evidence itself is uncertain. We introduce VisualNoiseQA, a novel benchmark for active reasoning under noisy visual feedback. A text-only LLM must solve VQA problems by iteratively querying a fixed, off-the-shelf VLM treated as a stochastic visual sensor. For each query, we draw multiple samples and expose an empirical uncertainty signal via self-consistency, enabling the reasoner to probe from different angles and decide what to ask next and when to stop. Our construction is automatic and scalable: starting from diverse VQA sources and two noisy VLMs, we retain only questions where the sensor is inconsistent yet human-solvable. We evaluate multiple LLM reasoners on 1,000 instances spanning perception, chart understanding, and knowledge-intensive reasoning. VisualNoiseQA thus provides a controlled playground to study how different LLMs exploit uncertainty signals for robust reasoning.

</details>


### [83] [ST-Bench: A Spatial-Temporal Benchmark for Multi-Agent System Generation on Scientific Research Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07763)
*Qi Cheng,Rongchao Dong,Shengyu Chen,Licheng Liu,Dan Lu,Zhengzhang Chen,Wei Cheng,Yiqun Xie,Haifeng Chen,Xiaowei Jia,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ST-Bench基准测试显示，基于大型语言模型的多智能体系统（MAS）在复杂科学数据分析任务上优于单智能体，主要体现在覆盖率提升而非单个输出质量的增强。最强的MAS配置综合得分接近最低成本单智能体的三倍，但推理时间约为四倍；更经济的流程能以不到两倍成本捕获大部分收益。这表明MAS在科学数据分析中具有条件性优势，而非普遍适用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多智能体系统（MAS）在具有真实数据、缺乏显式二进制反馈信号的科学数据分析领域，是否能在代码、数学或问答等已有benchmark中展现出的优势转化为实际的性能提升，并评估其性价比。

Method: 构建ST-Bench基准，包含100个源自地球科学研究的数据分析任务，扩展为2,067个查询并由领域专家验证。评估五种最新的MAS生成方法，在GPT-5骨干网络上与单智能体基线对比，采用两种训练协议进行衡量。

Result: 十种MAS配置中有九种超过最廉价的单智能体基线，最强配置综合得分接近其三倍。这种提升主要源于“覆盖率”：训练后的工作流能处理更多查询并产生真实的数值指标，而在产生有效输出的条件下，其质量与单智能体相当。最强配置需四倍推理时间，而经济型工作流以低于两倍成本获得大部分收益。

Conclusion: MAS专业化在科学数据分析中带来可测量的收益，但这种收益是条件性的（conditional），取决于具体的工作流设计和成本约束，并非 universally applicable。

Abstract: The rapid progress of LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) has shown that they largely outperform single agents on coding, math, and QA tasks, where executable tests provide a binary success signal. Whether this advantage transfers to real scientific data analysis remains untested. We introduce ST-Bench, a benchmark designed to answer two questions: whether MAS outperform single agents on complex scientific data analysis tasks, and if so, by how much and at what additional cost. ST-Bench contains 100 data science tasks adapted from published Earth science studies across hydrology, agriculture, and wetland methane research, expanded into 2,067 queries grounded in additional published studies and validated by domain experts. Using ST-Bench, we evaluate five recent MAS generation methods under two training protocols, against single-agent baselines on the same GPT-5 backbone. Nine of the ten MAS configurations exceed the cheapest single-agent baseline, with the strongest reaching nearly three times its composite score. This gain is primarily attributable to coverage: trained workflows produce realistic numerical metrics on a larger fraction of queries, while the quality of those metrics, conditional on producing realistic output, is comparable to that of the single-agent baseline. The strongest configuration requires approximately four times the single-agent inference time, whereas a more economical workflow captures the majority of the benefit at less than twice the cost. MAS specialization confers measurable benefit on scientific data analysis, but the benefit is conditional rather than universal.

</details>


### [84] [OTel: Open Telco AI Datasets, Benchmarks, and Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07766)
*Farbod Tavakkoli,Gregory Diamos,Kenneth Church,David Kanter,Mark Austin,Imtiaz Karim,Mirza Masfiqur Rahman,Merouane Abdelkader Debbah,Zeinab Nezami,Ali Maatouk,Leandros Tassiulas,Rex Ying,Nick Sorros,Louis Powell,Nikolaos Vasiloglou,Ashish Vaswani,Somanshu Singla,Adarsh Chaluvaraju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Open Telco (OTel) 是开放电信 AI 资源，提供数据集与30个预训练基准模型，显著提升检索、重排序及大语言模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 整合分散的电信数据与基准，提供统一、可复现的资源以支持检索、重排序、指令微调及安全评估，并促进社区协作。

Method: 发布包含文档化数据来源、保留验证集在内的电信数据集；基于开放权重模型进行后训练，涵盖10个嵌入模型、3个重排序器及17个语言模型，并采用开放训练配方。

Result: 检索 NDCG@10 达 93.1%，重排序 MRR@10 达 0.947，语言模型正确率达 87.8%；截至2026年5月，模型下载超1600万次，获157+媒体报道。

Conclusion: OTel 作为可复现起点有效提升了三类模型的电信领域表现，呼吁社区扩展数据并构建更强的上下文感知电信大模型。

Abstract: We present Open Telco (OTel), an open telecom AI resource that releases derived telecom datasets for retrieval, reranking, instruction tuning, and safety/abstention, together with 30 full-parameter post-trained baselines spanning 10 embedding models, 3 rerankers, and 17 language models. The community has already engaged substantially with the resource: as of May 3, 2026, the released models have been downloaded over 16 million times and the project has received 157+ pieces of media coverage worldwide. Building on prior open telecom datasets and benchmarks, OTel provides documented telecom data sources, held-out evaluation partitions, trained embedding models, rerankers, context-grounded LLMs, and safety/abstention data in one unified resource. Each baseline starts from an open-weight model and is post-trained on OTel-derived data using an open training recipe, then evaluated on held-out OTel evaluation partitions. OTel post-training improves performance across all three model families: embedding retrieval reaches 93.1% NDCG@10, reranking reaches 0.947 MRR@10, and language-model correctness reaches 87.8%. We release OTel as a reproducible starting point and invite the community to expand the data, improve embedding and reranking models, and build stronger context-grounded telecom LLMs.

</details>


### [85] [Quantization Effects on Tool-Failure Recovery Vary Across Prompts and Evaluation Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07781)
*Yuhe Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 后训练量化模型（4-bit vs 8-bit）在工具恢复能力上的表现高度依赖于评估设置（提示词、任务集、评分策略）。单一维度的评估无法得出稳定结论，建议采用匹配任务的全流水线成功率并报告不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练量化旨在降低语言模型智能体的部署成本，但其在应对临时工具故障时的鲁棒性恢复效果尚未明确，且现有评估方法可能存在偏差，导致结果不一致。

Method: 对比 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 和 Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct 的 8-bit 与 4-bit 变体，在二十个确定性工具使用任务和五个提示词下进行测试。研究分析了不同评分策略（如仅限自身通过的任务、全流水线评分）和执行宽容度对结果的影响。

Result: 8-bit 与 4-bit 的恢复优势因提示词和目标而异，方向经常反转。例如，Llama 在全流水线评分下始终 favor 8-bit，但在特定筛选任务集下可能 favor 4-bit；评分标准的严格程度（如输出解析）也可显著改变优劣判断（如从 +28.3 变为 -15.0）。

Conclusion: 仅凭单一提示词、筛选后的任务集或固定的评分政策无法确立关于量化智能体鲁棒性的稳定结论。推荐评估应使用匹配任务集，报告全流水线成功率以辅助部署决策，明确评分政策，并量化任务间的不确定性而非注入故障点。

Abstract: Post-training quantization reduces the cost of deploying language-model agents, but its effect on recovery from temporary tool failures can depend on how recovery is evaluated. We compare 8-bit and 4-bit variants of Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct on twenty deterministic tool-use tasks and five prompts. The 8-bit-4-bit recovery comparison changes direction across prompts and evaluation targets. On tasks that both variants complete without faults under the same prompt, the difference ranges from 0 to +20.2 percentage points for Llama and from -50.0 to +35.0 points for Qwen. Full-pipeline point estimates favor 8-bit Llama under all five prompts, whereas the Qwen comparison changes direction across prompts. The evaluation target can also reverse the result. For Llama under one prompt, scoring each variant only on its own clean-passing tasks favors 4-bit by 17.5 points; scoring the same tasks for both variants gives no difference, while scoring the full pipeline favors 8-bit by 28.3 points. Executor leniency is a third such choice. Rescoring the same logs with strict output parsing, which 8-bit Llama violates far more often than 4-bit Llama under that prompt, turns that +28.3 into -15.0 while leaving Qwen essentially unchanged. These findings show that one prompt, one screened task set, and one scoring policy do not establish a stable conclusion about quantized-agent robustness. Evaluations should compare variants on matched tasks, report full-pipeline success for deployment decisions, state the scoring policy, and quantify uncertainty across tasks rather than injected fault sites.

</details>


### [86] [Persistent Memory in Multi-Agent LLM Inference: What It Costs, What It Buys, and When You Can Tell](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07782)
*Hochan Son,Kyungdoe Han,Jaehan Koh,Xiaowu Dai,Wenlu Xu,Guang Cheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究挑战了在三层级代理架构中引入“持久化记忆层”以增强长上下文推理有效性的观点。通过严格控制实验，发现该记忆层不仅未能提升准确率（精度增益统计不显著），反而增加了峰值KV缓存开销。作者指出之前的准确性声称源于基准测试设计的结构性缺陷和测量修正不当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管许多多智能体系统假设持久化记忆层能显著提高推理准确性并据此进行消融实验验证，但现有评估方法可能存在偏差。本研究旨在客观评估在受限KV缓存资源下，分解推理与持久化记忆层的真实性能影响及准确性增益，纠正此前可能被夸大的效能认知。

Method: 1. **架构评估**：在一个三层级代理架构上，对比分解式推理、单遍处理及检索增强基线。
2. **测量指标**：监测峰值工作集KV缓存大小（单位MiB）和准确性变化。
3. **对照实验**：使用8对受控数据集，每组n=100样本，严格比较有无持久化记忆层的差异。
4. **误差分析**：识别导致之前结果偏差的四种测量修正错误（3种夸大收益，1种掩盖不可解决的效果）。
5. **提出标准**：定义代理记忆消融实验必须满足的条件及检测程序。

Result: 1. **内存效率**：分解式推理显著降低峰值KV缓存需求至14.3 MiB/查询，优于单遍（35.5 MiB）和检索增强（35.3 MiB）。
2. **无效的记忆增益**：持久化记忆层导致峰值缓存增加约+0.368 MiB，但在准确性上无检测到的变化（均值+0.015，95% CI [-0.011, +0.046]）。
3. **结论归因**：“零结果”是由于单问题基准测试的结构性缺陷：每个项目独立评分且证据隔离，存储的推理痕迹在无状态重置间无法提供可检索的信息。

Conclusion: 持久化记忆层在当前设定下并未带来预期的准确性收益，反而增加了内存负担。此前的准确性增益报告可能源于测量方法的系统性偏差或基准测试设计的结构性无效。研究提出了更严谨的代理记忆消融实验条件和检测程序，以确保未来评估的可靠性。

Abstract: Decomposing long-context inference across cooperating agents bounds the active KV cache per call rather than total evidence, which matters when KV-cache memory binds. Many such systems add a persistent tier storing and recalling reasoning traces, usually validated by an ablation reporting an accuracy gain. We measure both on one three-tier agent architecture. Decomposition delivers: peak KV working set of 14.3 MiB per query against 35.5 and 35.3 MiB for single-pass and retrieval-augmented baselines. The persistent tier does not: across eight controlled dataset pairs at n=100 per arm it costs +0.368 MiB [+0.167, +0.590] of peak cache and produces no detectable accuracy change (+0.015, 95% CI [-0.011, +0.046]). We argue the null is structural: single-question benchmarks supply each item with its own evidence and score it independently, and correctness requires resetting stored traces between conditions, so recall has nothing informative to retrieve. Reaching it took four measurement corrections -- three inflating the apparent benefit, the fourth making an effect that size look resolvable -- none visible in the results table. We give the conditions an agent-memory ablation must satisfy and detection procedures that need no knowledge of the specific defect.

</details>


### [87] [Attacca: Goal-Directed Control under State Continuity for Long-Horizon Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07785)
*Gyusik Seo,Jaehong Yoon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Attacca，一种通过在完整搜索-交互轨迹上训练视觉目标条件策略来解决具身代理在长视域任务中难以定位目标的问题。该方法结合上下文解耦目标采样、目标掩码预测和行为阶段 conditioning，显著提升了Minecraft环境中短和长视域任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉目标条件策略通常在目标可见的孤立交互场景下评估，无法捕捉连续长时任务执行中因前置任务导致的姿态变化、环境改变及目标超出视野等挑战，导致代理在无法在当前观察中定位目标时难以继续后续任务。

Method: 提出Attarca方法：1）使用从执行环境中解耦的目标图像进行上下文解耦目标采样，将演示与来自其他世界但类别兼容的掩码目标图像配对；2）通过目标掩码预测头学习密集当前视角的地面真值，提供动作模仿之外的辅助监督；3）引入行为阶段条件机制，使策略能区分搜索（Search）、接近（Approach）和交互（Interact）阶段并自适应调整控制。

Result: 在Minecraft的多个短期和长期具身任务上评估，Attarca实现了39.0-47.5%的纯净成功率，优于最强基线1.7-2.4倍；在长期任务中达到54%、30%和28%的完成率，最高提升达7倍。

Conclusion: Attacca通过解耦目标采样和阶段性行为控制，有效解决了长时任务中的目标定位难题，大幅提升了具身代理在多阶段复杂任务中的执行能力。

Abstract: A central capability of embodied agents is to accomplish complex objectives through sequences of interdependent tasks. Yet existing visual goal-conditioned policies underlying these agents are typically evaluated on isolated interactions where the target is already visible, and thus do not capture the conditions that arise during continuous long-horizon task execution. In such settings, each task begins from the state left by the previous one: the agent may end at a different position and orientation, the world may have been modified, and the next interaction target may lie outside the current field of view. As a result, agents relying on such policies may struggle to proceed to the next task when they cannot ground their target in the current observation. To address this challenge, we propose Attacca, a new approach that trains visual goal-conditioned policies on complete search-to-interact trajectories using goal images decoupled from the execution environment. Attacca uses context-decoupled goal sampling to pair each demonstration with a class-compatible masked goal image from another world, removing direct scene and pose correspondence. It learns dense current-view grounding through a target-mask prediction head, providing auxiliary supervision beyond action imitation. We further introduce behavioral-phase conditioning that teaches the policy to distinguish Search, Approach, and Interact stages and adapt its control as execution progresses. We evaluate Attacca on multiple short- and long-horizon embodied tasks in Minecraft. Our method achieves 39.0-47.5% clean success, improving over the strongest baseline by 1.7-2.4x. On long-horizon tasks, it attains 54%, 30%, and 28% completion, yielding up to a 7x improvement.

</details>


### [88] [OOPMAS: Object-Oriented Multi-Agent Systems for Query-Level Workflow Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07787)
*Qi Cheng,Shengyu Chen,Wei Cheng,Yiqun Xie,Xiaowei Jia,Haoyu Wang,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OOPMAS，一种无需训练的框架，针对单个查询动态生成智能体和工作流，利用动态技能库实现上下文改进，在混合任务基准上准确率显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化多智能体系统（MAS）设计主要在任务层面运行，为每个基准测试生成单一固定的工作流并统一应用于所有查询。这种假设不符合现实情况，因为同一任务内的查询难度差异巨大，且真实世界的工作负载通常混合了异构的任务类型。

Method: 介绍了一种名为OOPMAS的训练自由框架，其在单个查询粒度上生成智能体集合和协调工作流。智能体被表示为具有特定角色、工具和持久状态的对象导向类定义，工作流则表达为这些智能体对象上的可执行主函数。通过执行反馈积累结构化教训的动态技能库，实现了无需梯度更新或微调的上下文改进。

Result: 在涵盖代码、数学和问答的混合任务基准测试中，OOPMAS实现了89.6%的准确率，比最强的基线高出18.1个百分点。跨四种大语言模型后端的模型交换研究显示出一致的扩展性，在使用最强模型时准确率达到92.4%。

Conclusion: OOPMAS通过细粒度的动态生成的智能体和工作流机制，结合无梯度的上下文学习改进，显著提升了多智能体系统在复杂多变现实场景下的性能表现。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) powered by large language models have shown strong performance across code generation, mathematical reasoning, and question answering. However, existing methods for automating MAS design mostly operate at the task level, producing a single fixed workflow per benchmark that is applied uniformly to all queries. This assumption fails under realistic conditions. Query difficulty varies widely within a task, and real-world workloads mix heterogeneous task types. We introduce OOPMAS, a training-free framework that generates both the agent set and the coordination workflow at the granularity of individual queries. Agents are represented as object-oriented class definitions with dedicated roles, tools, and persistent state, and workflows are expressed as executable main functions over these agent objects. A dynamic skill library accumulates structured lessons from execution feedback across optimization rounds, enabling in-context improvement without any gradient updates or fine-tuning. On a mixed-task benchmark of queries spanning code, math, and QA, OOPMAS achieves 89.6% accuracy, outperforming the strongest baseline by 18.1 percentage points. A model-swap study across four LLM backbones shows consistent scaling, reaching 92.4% with the strongest model.

</details>


### [89] [Illusory Pattern Perception Drives Spurious Inference in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07791)
*Peihua Mai,Zhuoyan Shao,Xinbao Qiao,Meng Zhang,Xinyue Zhou,Yan Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究大型语言模型（LLM）中存在的“虚幻模式感知”现象，发现LLM比人类更容易产生此类认知错觉。通过稀疏自编码器（SAEs）分析，揭示了频率感知和认知取向与这种错觉的内在联系，警示这可能影响LLM推理的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM是否表现出类似于人类的“虚幻模式感知”（即在随机数据中推断不存在的有意义关系）这一认知倾向，因为这种倾向可能导致下游应用中产生系统性的推理错误。

Method: 1. 将经典心理学范式改编为三项任务，对LLM与人类行为进行直接的实证比较；
2. 开发基于稀疏自编码器（Sparse Autoencoders, SAEs）的特征可解释性框架，以分析LLM的内部表示机制。

Result: 1. LLM常表现出比人类更强的虚幻模式感知能力；
2. 模型倾向于将高频正面属性与多数群体或大型组织过度关联；
3. 模型在从模糊事件中构建因果叙事时表现出更强的倾向性；
4. “整体频率感知”和“分析性认知取向”内部特征与虚幻感知的出现密切相关。

Conclusion: LLM中存在一种以前未被充分探索的类认知错觉（虚幻模式感知），这可能会损害LLM推理的可靠性，需引起重视并在后续应用中进行考量。

Abstract: Illusory pattern perception is a well-documented human cognitive tendency to infer meaningful relationships in data that is actually random. Such a tendency, often described as "connecting the dots" where none exist, can result in systematic reasoning errors. This paper investigates whether Large Language Models (LLMs) exhibit such perceptual tendencies, which can lead to systematic errors in downstream applications. To our knowledge, this work presents the first systematic study of illusory pattern perception in LLMs, adapting classic psychological paradigms to three tasks with direct empirical comparison to human behaviors. We find that LLMs frequently exhibit stronger illusory pattern perception than humans. In particular, models tend to over-associate frequent positive attributes with majority groups or large organizations, and show increased tendencies to construct causal narratives from ambiguous events. To uncover the mechanism behind these behaviors, we develop a feature interpretability framework based on Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) to analyze internal representations. Our results reveal that holistic frequency perception and analytic cognitive orientation are linked to the emergence of illusory perceptions. These findings highlight a previously underexplored cognitive-like illusion that may affect the reliability of LLM reasoning. Code available at https://github.com/NusIoraPrivacy/illusory.

</details>


### [90] [Thin Evidence, Thick Priors: How Language Models Substitute Identity for Missing Financial Facts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07798)
*Saanvi Khetan,Sankar Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现大型语言模型（LLM）在缺乏完整财务信息时，会受提示词中“投资者身份”的显著影响。随着披露事实减少，性别、家庭规模等人口统计特征对推荐资产配置的影响力急剧上升，甚至导致模型“幻觉”出未提供的财务细节。研究建议审计应模拟真实低披露场景，并全面评估身份偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户常向大语言模型咨询理财建议但很少提供完整的财务状况。本文旨在探究当财务信息缺失时，模型如何处理这种信息缺口，特别是身份属性（persona）对建议结果的影响程度。

Method: 使用Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct模型，生成包含96,600个提示词的实验集。通过固定财务背景、仅改变“声称的投资者身份”，并在100个财务档案、138个人设和7种披露条件下，从完整披露（8个事实）到完全无财务事实进行梯度测试。采用双路聚类Bootstrap方法统计显著性，并分析网络内部的可解码性及消融实验。

Result: 1. **身份影响力剧增**：在全披露情况下，相同财务背景下不同人设间的平均股权分配差异为4.78%；在无财务事实时，这一差距扩大至10.34%。无披露下身份解释了96%的建议变化，而全披露下仅为5%。
2. **关键属性**：家庭规模是唯一影响力随证据减少而可靠增长的属性；性别在全披露不足时表现为微小的恒定差距。
3. **幻觉现象**：在无事实输入时，模型会编造收入、债务和储蓄等它从未被告知的财务细节来支撑其推理，且这些虚构细节往往对大家庭不利。
4. **技术洞察**：性别在网络各层均线性可解，但消融性别方向并未改变整体的身份波动幅度。仅声明风险偏好即可产生与多数通用事实相似的影响范围。

Conclusion: 基于此类模型的咨询系统必须在模拟用户实际达到的低披露水平下进行审计，而非仅在理想的全信息假设下测试。评估应覆盖整个身份空间，而非单一属性，以揭示潜在的身份偏见和信息幻觉风险。

Abstract: People increasingly ask large language models what to do with their money, yet seldom describe their finances in full. This paper asks what a model does with the gap. Holding finances fixed and changing only who the investor is said to be, we grade the financial evidence in the prompt from eight facts to none and measure how far the recommended equity allocation moves. Across 96,600 prompts to Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, built from 100 financial profiles, 138 personas and seven disclosure conditions, the average gap between two personas with identical finances rises from 4.78 percentage points at full disclosure to 10.34 points with no financial facts. A two-way cluster bootstrap counting duplicated prompts once places the ratio at 2.16 (95% interval 1.69 to 2.79), and the rise is already 1.69-fold with a single fact left. Identity explains 5% of within-profile variation in advice at full disclosure and 96% with no disclosure. Household size is the only attribute whose influence grows reliably as evidence is withdrawn. Once standard errors are clustered on the persona, the unit to which identity was assigned, most attribute-specific interactions reported in the conference version lose significance, and gender instead appears as a small standing gap that full disclosure does not close. Stating risk appetite alone brings the swing into the range seen with two to seven generic facts. With no facts, the model's one-line rationale cites incomes, debts and savings it was never told, and these invented finances turn adverse more often for larger households. Inside the network, gender is linearly decodable at every layer, and ablating the gender direction at five layers leaves the aggregate identity swing unchanged. Advisory systems built on such models should be audited at the disclosure levels users actually reach, and judged across the whole identity space rather than one attribute at a time.

</details>


### [91] [ThinkFuse: Trajectory-Aware Test-Time Fusion for Small Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07803)
*Myunghoon Kang,Jungseob Lee,Jaehyung Seo,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ThinkFuse，一种免训练的测试时融合框架，通过对比片段级与轨迹级不确定性来识别并修复推理错误路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型推理模型(SRMs)虽能处理复杂任务但易陷入错误路径且难以恢复；现有基于局部信号的融合方法易受波动误导并强化不稳定轨迹。

Method: ThinkFuse框架选择性干预不可靠推理片段：通过比较片段级不确定性变化与轨迹级趋势定位不稳定点，并将辅助推理路径融入主模型轨迹。

Result: 在数学和知识密集型基准上优于基线，跨模型族组合表现稳健且在小主模型下有效；分析显示其触发次数少、生成Token少，具高效率。

Conclusion: ThinkFuse通过选择性触发机制有效提升了推理稳定性与效率，代码已开源。

Abstract: Small reasoning models (SRMs) have shown strong performance on complex reasoning tasks by generating extended chain-of-thought trajectories, but they often fail to recover once their reasoning enters an erroneous path. Existing test-time fusion methods rely on local fusion signals to determine when to trigger fusion, which can be misled by transient uncertainty fluctuations and may reinforce unstable reasoning trajectories. We propose ThinkFuse, a training-free test-time fusion framework that selectively intervenes in unreliable reasoning segments. ThinkFuse compares segment-level uncertainty shifts with trajectory-level uncertainty trends to identify unstable reasoning points and fuse auxiliary reasoning paths into the primary model's trajectory. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ThinkFuse outperforms baselines on mathematical and knowledge-intensive reasoning benchmarks, with consistent gains across model-family combinations, and remains robust with a smaller primary model. Our analysis shows that ThinkFuse requires fewer fusion triggers and generates fewer tokens, highlighting the efficiency of selective triggering. Our code is available at https://github.com/js-lee-AI/ThinkFuse.

</details>


### [92] [Do I Need the Cloud? Uncertainty-Aware Step-Level Handoff for Small Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07816)
*Abolfazl Younesi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出STEPGATE，一种基于不确定性的逐步迁移框架，用于在小型语言模型（SLM）和更强模型之间进行动态切换，以解决代理步骤失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型虽因降低延迟和部署成本而具吸引力，但易受结构化工具错误影响；现有路由器仅按查询选择模型，无法应对代理决策中动态变化的难度。

Method: 设计STEPGATE框架，对每个本地SLM动作评分，并将困难步骤选择性升级至更强模型，实现基于步骤的不确定性感知转移。

Result: 在单步测试中，Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B配对达到82.7%任务成功率（30.8%升级率），优于本地-only（67.3%）和随机升级（75.4%）；在多轮评估中，轨迹成功率69.0%，动作成功率84.0%，云操作仅占30.0%，性能接近强模型后端。

Conclusion: 步骤级升级能以较低的远程传输token量显著缩小与更强模型的性能差距，但当前评估局限于单一模型族、脚本任务和较小数据集，风险分级仅为研究标注而非正式安全保证。

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) are attractive as local agent controllers because they reduce remote inference, latency, and deployment footprint, yet structured tool errors can cause an agent step to fail. Existing routers typically select a model once per query. However, agents expose sequential decision points whose difficulty dynamically changes based on intermediate observations. We propose STEPGATE, an uncertainty-aware handoff framework that scores each local SLM action and selectively escalates challenging steps to a stronger model. On a 52-task held-out single-step BFCL-derived test split, the Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B pair attains 82.7% task success with 30.8% escalation, versus 67.3% local-only and 75.4% random escalation (which uses 33.8% escalation). In a separate multi-turn evaluation, STEPGATE achieves 69.0% trajectory success and 84.0% action success using only 30.0% cloud actions, compared with 48.0%/70.5% local-only, 60.0%/78.2% random escalation, and 57.0%/77.1% query-level routing (strong-only achieves 82.0% trajectory success at 100% cloud actions). These results suggest that step-level escalation recovers a large share of the performance gap to the stronger Qwen2.5-7B backend at a matched cloud-action rate while transmitting fewer tokens remotely. However, our evaluation is limited to one model family, a single stronger backend, and scripted tasks. Furthermore, the test sets are small, multi-turn comparisons rely on paired intervals and statistical tests, and our risk tiers serve as research annotations rather than formal safety guarantees.

</details>


### [93] [Agentic Semantic Sensing for Resource-Adaptive AI-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07829)
*Zhongqin Wang,Xiaoqi Zhang,Nan Yang,Kai Wu,J. Andrew Zhang,Y. Jay Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Agentic SemS，一种用于AI-RANs的闭环语义感知框架。通过Profile-conditioned因果Transformer和KV缓存更新语义信念，利用语义效用网络评估观测收益并指导自适应配置与早期退出。相比全序列方法，降低25.33%感知成本，宏观F1达85.79%；在相同效用阈值下，早期退出进一步降低成本12.35%，性能损失仅0.97个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义感知（SemS）多为开环机制，其感知配置和观察计划固定，无法根据任务演变的证据进行动态响应。

Method: 构建闭环控制框架：使用Profile-conditioned因果Transformer从流式数据中更新语义信念，并利用键值缓存高效处理状态更新；建立语义效用网络估计任务级收益，结合感知成本进行下一个可行profile的选择及语义早期退出决策。

Result: 在Widar3.0数据集上的实验显示，该资源高效设置比全序列High方案降低归一化累积感知成本25.33%，Macro-F1为85.79%。在同等效用检查点，语义早期退出比无早期退出的自适应感知再降成本12.35%，Macro-F1仅下降0.97%。

Conclusion: 所提Agentic SemS实现了通信可行的闭环语义感知，将预期语义增益与条件互信息关联以解释信息价值，显著优化了感知成本与性能的平衡。

Abstract: Semantic sensing (SemS) acquires task-relevant information rather than reconstructing complete physical information. Existing SemS formulations typically operate open loop: sensing configurations and observation schedules are fixed before inference and cannot respond to evolving task-level evidence. We propose Agentic SemS, a closed-loop framework for AI-enabled radio access networks (AI-RANs) that controls sensing within a communication-feasible profile set. A profile-conditioned causal Transformer updates the semantic belief from streaming observations, while key-value caching enables efficient state updates across profile changes without repeatedly processing the complete history. A semantic utility network estimates the task-level benefit of acquiring the next observation block under each feasible profile after accounting for sensing cost. The resulting continuation utilities jointly support next-profile selection and semantic early exit, adapting sensing configuration and duration to evolving evidence. The expected semantic gain is further related to conditional mutual information, providing a value-of-information interpretation of continued online sensing. Experiments on Widar3.0 with six emulated sensing profiles show that, in comparison with full-sequence High, the resource-efficient Agentic setting reduces normalized cumulative sensing cost by 25.33% while achieving 85.79% Macro-F1. At the same utility checkpoint, semantic early exit provides a further 12.35% cost reduction over adaptive sensing without early exit, with a 0.97-percentage-point Macro-F1 decrease.

</details>


### [94] [DHCG: Dynamic Construction of Hierarchical Collaboration Graphs for LLM-Based Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07835)
*Jie Ren,Jiakang Yuan,Chenyu Huang,Hezeer Ma,Jiayuan Fan,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DHCG框架，将多智能体系统设计为部分可观察马尔可夫决策过程，通过动态构建层级协作图解决传统方法在组合、依赖和扩展性上的局限。实验表明其在代码生成、数学推理等任务上达到SOTA，显著优于单智能体及静态/动态多智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的多智能体系统（MAS）在动态编排方面存在组成受限、依赖错配和扩展不灵活等问题，难以适应执行过程中的推理需求变化。

Method: 提出DHCG框架，包含规划器（Planner）、工作者（Worker）和生成器（Generator）三个模块。通过将MAS设计为部分可观察马尔可夫决策过程，利用规划器根据查询和执行反馈逐步生成互补角色并路由信息，动态构建或终止层级协作图。引入动作感知偏好优化以训练规划器做出更有效的决策。

Result: 在代码生成、数学推理和领域特定推理基准测试中，DHCG取得最先进的平均性能。相比单智能体基线提升13.06分，相比静态和动态MAS基线提升2.77-8.02分。具备对不同规划器骨干网络和未见工作者模型的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 动态层级协作图机制能有效解决多智能体系统的自适应难题，显著提升复杂推理任务的 performance 和灵活性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have demonstrated strong capabilities in solving complex problems across diverse domains. Recently, the dynamic orchestration of agent systems has become an important research direction. However, existing methods suffer from limited composition, misaligned dependencies, and inflexible scale, restricting their ability to adapt to reasoning requirements during execution. To address these limitations, we reframe MAS design as a partially observable Markov decision process, in which both the composition and scale of the MAS are dynamically determined. We propose DHCG, a novel framework that coordinates three modules (Planner, Worker, and Generator) to progressively construct a dynamic hierarchical collaboration graph from scratch based on the query and evolving execution feedback. At each step, guided by feedback, the Planner generates a set of distinct and complementary roles tailored to the current reasoning needs and selectively routes relevant information to each role. It can also finalize the hierarchical collaboration graph early or progressively expand it when additional reasoning is required. We further introduce action-aware preference optimization to train the Planner to make more effective decisions when constructing hierarchical collaboration graphs. We systematically evaluate DHCG across code generation, mathematical reasoning, and domain-specific reasoning benchmarks. DHCG achieves state-of-the-art average performance among the compared methods, improving over the single-agent baseline by 13.06 points and outperforming both static and dynamic MAS baselines by 2.77-8.02 points. Additional experiments further demonstrate its generalization across different Planner backbones and unseen Worker models.

</details>


### [95] [RA-MoWE: Workflow-Affinity Embeddings for Query Clustering and Agentic Workflow Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07851)
*Qi Cheng,Shengyu Chen,Wei Cheng,Yiqun Xie,Haoyu Wang,Haifeng Chen,Xiaowei Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RA-MoWE框架，通过工作流亲和嵌入聚类查询并生成可复用专家工作流，平衡了整体优化与个体差异，在数学、科学和编程任务上提升4.04%准确率并减少27.7%的LLM调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有L代理工作流的权衡困境：全局优化单一工作流会忽略个体查询所需的特定推理策略差异，而为每个查询单独搜索新工作流则导致极高的计算成本。

Method: 引入RA-MoWE框架，利用“工作流亲和嵌入”（workflow-affinity embeddings）将查询聚类，以指导可重用专家工作流的生成；通过执行反馈初始化并精炼专用工作流；使用嵌入编码器从查询文本直接预测嵌入，从而避免在执行参考工作流前进行昂贵的搜索。

Result: 在涵盖数学、科学和编程领域的300个查询测试集上，RA-MoWE比直接选择参考工作流平均提升4.04个百分点的任务得分，同时在推理阶段减少了27.7%的语言模型调用次数。

Conclusion: 基于嵌入聚类的代理工作流方法能够有效平衡通用性与个性化需求，显著提升了复杂任务解决的效率与效果，实现了更智能的资源分配。

Abstract: Agentic workflows enable large language models (LLMs) to solve complex tasks by coordinating reasoning, tool use, and verification. However, a workflow optimized for an entire task collection can overlook differences in the reasoning strategies that individual queries need, while searching for a new workflow for every query repeats costly optimization. To address this tradeoff, we introduce RA-MoWE, a framework that uses workflow-affinity embeddings to cluster queries and guide the generation of reusable expert workflows. Each embedding records how well a fixed set of reference workflows solves a query, revealing similarities in which reasoning strategies are effective. RA-MoWE uses each cluster's queries and average embedding to initialize and refine a specialized workflow through execution feedback. An embedding encoder predicts these embeddings from query text, allowing new queries to select a generated expert without first executing the reference workflows. On a 300-query test set drawn from four benchmarks spanning mathematics, science, and programming, RA-MoWE improves average task score by 4.04 percentage points over selecting among the reference workflows, while using 27.7% fewer language-model calls at inference.

</details>


### [96] [WorkflowOps: Learning Agent Collaboration Priors for Multi-Agent Workflow Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07860)
*Qi Cheng,Shengyu Chen,Wei Cheng,Zhengzhang Chen,Xiaowei Jia,Haoyu Wang,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorkflowOps是一个多智能体工作流编排框架，通过从历史流程中学习协作先验知识和按需扩展智能体池，解决了传统系统“无记忆”的问题。它利用转移概率矩阵指导DAG构建、通过语义匹配发现能力缺口并生成新智能体、以及采用分层语义匹配策略显著减少LLM调用，从而在代码、数学和问答任务上提升了端到端通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体系统在处理复杂知识工作时，其编排层大多是“无记忆”的，即每次新任务都从头开始分解和执行，无法利用之前成功执行的经验，导致效率低下且缺乏协同优化。

Method: 提出了三种耦合机制：1) 转移概率矩阵机制：捕捉过去工作流中的智能体成对协作频率，并在DAG工作流构建中作为软引导，通过层内排序优化、概率阈值边建议和传递闭包约简来最大化并行性；2) 充足性驱动的智能体创建循环：通过语义匹配分数检测能力缺口，利用LLM生成专用智能体并将其注入协作矩阵，使其具备预测的协作先验知识；3) 分层语义匹配策略：使用预训练句子嵌入进行快速确定性的能力匹配，仅在低置信度情况下调用LLM验证，以减少LLM路由调用。

Result: 在混合代码、数学和问题回答套件上的实验表明，WorkflowOps比最近的工作流构建基线提高了端到端的通过率，特别是在具有结构化、可分解特性的任务上，由于过去的智能体交接模式可以迁移，获得的增益最大。此外，该策略将LLM路由调用减少了80%以上。

Conclusion: WorkflowOps通过引入学习到的协作先验知识和动态能力扩展机制，有效解决了多智能体编排的记忆缺失问题，在保持高效并行性和降低计算成本的同时，显著提升了复杂任务的处理性能。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed for complex knowledge work, yet their orchestration layers remain largely memoryless: each new task is decomposed, assigned, and executed from scratch with no benefit from prior successful executions. We present WorkflowOps, a multi-agent workflow orchestration framework that learns agent collaboration priors from historical workflows and expands its agent pool on demand to cover new capability requirements. Our approach introduces three coupled mechanisms. First, a transition probability matrix captures pairwise agent collaboration frequencies from past workflows and applies them as soft guidance during DAG workflow construction through intra-layer ordering optimization, probability-thresholded edge suggestion, and transitive reduction for parallelism maximization. Second, a sufficiency-driven agent creation loop detects capability gaps via semantic matching scores, generates specialized agents through an LLM, and simultaneously injects them into the collaboration matrix, so that newly created agents are immediately usable with predicted collaboration priors. Third, a layered semantic matching strategy uses pre-trained sentence embeddings for fast, deterministic capability matching as a first pass, invoking LLM verification only for low-confidence cases, thereby reducing LLM routing calls by over 80\% compared to pure-LLM approaches. Experiments on mixed code, math, and question-answering suites show that WorkflowOps improves end-to-end pass rates over recent workflow-construction baselines, with the largest gains on structured, decomposable tasks where past agent handoff patterns transfer.

</details>


### [97] [Self-Referenced Social Preferences: Cooperation without Observing Others Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07881)
*Mohamed Ayman Mohamed,Harshil Kotamreddy,Marcos Menon Jose*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出自引用社会偏好（self-referenced social preferences），使智能体无需观测同行私有奖励信号，仅通过观察行为和结果即可学习合作行为。该方法在三种环境实验中证明了其有效性及公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体强化学习方法通常要求代理观察同伴的奖励，但在现实世界中，智能体往往只能观察他人的行为和结果，而无法获取其私有的奖励信号。旨在解决这一信息不对称问题。

Method: 引入“自引用社会偏好”：每个智能体学习自身奖励模型，并应用于其他智能体观察到的转移状态，从而从自身视角评估他者结果。将该自引用评估融入标准社会偏好中，通过两种机制整合：修改学习奖励或加权策略更新。

Result: 在Escape Room、Clean Up和Commons Harvest三个 sequential social dilemmas 环境中验证。智能体在不观测他人奖励的情况下成功学会合作，甚至在独立学习者无法合作的场景下也能实现。与拥有真实奖励信息的智能体相比，常能达成更公平的收益分配。具体集成方式因社会偏好类型而异：厌恶不平等最适合结合带有价值前瞻的奖励修改，而纯粹的利他偏好则受益于策略更新加权。

Conclusion: 明确的同行奖励信号访问对于学习合作行为并非必要条件。社会偏好可以通过基于观察行为推导出的对他者结果的自引用评估来建立基础，即使在部分可观察性条件下该方法依然有效支持合作。

Abstract: Social preferences can promote cooperation in multi-agent reinforcement learning, but existing approaches often require agents to observe the rewards of their peers. In many real-world interactions, however, an agent can, as humans do, observe others' behavior and outcomes without access to their private reward signals. We introduce self-referenced social preferences, in which each agent learns a model of its own reward, applies it to other agents' observed transitions to assess their outcomes from its own perspective, and feeds these self-referenced assessments into standard social preferences. We study two ways to incorporate these assessments: modifying the learning reward, or using them to weight policy updates. We evaluate the approach on three sequential social dilemmas, Escape Room, Clean Up, and Commons Harvest, which require volunteering, public-good contribution, and resource restraint, respectively. Across all three environments, agents learn cooperative behavior without observing others' rewards, including in settings where independent learners fail to cooperate, and frequently achieve more equitable divisions of jointly produced returns than agents with access to true rewards. The effective integration point depends on the social preference: inequity aversion works best in the reward together with a value look-ahead, whereas a purely benevolent preference benefits from policy-update weighting. Under partial observability, the policy-update approach continues to support cooperation. These results show that explicit access to other agents' reward signals is not necessary for learning cooperative behavior: social preferences can instead be grounded in self-referenced assessments of others' outcomes derived from their observed behavior.

</details>


### [98] [ShanLiangRen: A Nutrition Agent for Personalized Daily Meal Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07886)
*Miao Xie,Xiao Zhang,Yuan Wang,Ruixin Zhu,Chunli Lv*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种个性化的多目标饮食规划问题（MDP），并开发了名为ShanLiangRen的AI代理来解决该问题。该系统通过检索增强生成缩小候选范围，并利用LLM进行帕累托原则指导的细化，最终输出精确到食材和份量的量化饮食计划及营养合规报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的通用饮食指南难以满足个人化约束与多维营养目标的冲突，且用户需求随反馈动态变化，导致通用指南与实际可执行计划之间存在巨大差距。旨在解决慢性病管理和健康维护中个性化、可执行的饮食规划难题。

Method: 1. 定义个性化完全量化多目标饮食规划问题（MDP）。2. 开发ShanLiangRen营养代理：首先将用户自然语言需求、属性及营养素数据转化为个体化约束规划实例；其次采用精确检索增强生成方法从庞大的食材和食谱库中筛选可行候选集；最后利用大语言模型（LLM）结合确定性营养计算和约束验证反馈，基于帕累托原则对候选计划进行迭代优化。3. 部署为微信小程序“ ShanLiangRen”。

Result: 系统能够输出完全量化的膳食计划，明确包含食材种类和份量，并提供显示约束满足情况和营养区间达标情况的健康报告。系统已在线发布，可通过微信使用，相关演示视频已公开。

Conclusion: 该方法有效弥合了通用指南与可执行计划之间的差距，实现了在满足个性化复杂约束的同时达成多维营养目标的自动化、精准化饮食规划。

Abstract: Dietary nutrition planning plays an important role in chronic disease management and maintaining a healthy body. In applications, it must simultaneously satisfy personalized constraints and reasonable multidimensional nutritional goals. These two aspects often conflict, and user constraints evolve with feedback, resulting in a substantial gap between generic guidelines and executable plans. To bridge this gap, we first propose the personalized fully quantified multiobjective dietary planning problem (MDP). To tackle MDP, we develop a nutrition agent, ShanLiangRen. The system first transforms dietary specifications, nutrient data, user attributes and natural language requirements into an individualized constrained planning instance. It then employs an exact retrieval-augmented generation method to shrink the feasible candidate set from a large scale ingredient and recipe space. Finally, it adopts a refinement guided by Pareto principles, where an LLM iteratively revises candidate plans under deterministic nutrition computation and feedback from constraint verification. The system outputs fully quantified meal plans with explicit ingredients and portion sizes, together with reports on nutrition compliance that show constraint satisfaction and nutrient interval attainment. We have released the system online as a WeChat Program, ShanLiangRen. A demo video is available at https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=652OtY5VlGA.

</details>


### [99] [Textual Environmental Context and Spatial Graphs for LLM-Based Regional SST Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07895)
*Xiong Li,Xiaowei Zhou,Yanwei Yu,Qian Cui,Junyu Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合大语言模型（LLM）、图神经网络（GNN）和连续图前缀的南海海温多步预测框架，通过条件数值生成实现高精度预报并附带基于知识的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在利用大语言模型进行区域多步海温预测，同时避免将完整的海温网格序列化为文本，以处理局部时间持久性、区域空间依赖性和随日期演变的动态环境条件。

Method: 将预测建模为条件数值生成：1）构建文本上下文，包含历史海温序列、异常值、日期对齐的环境记录及静态海洋知识；2）使用连续图推导前输入表示区域空间状态，其中静态图编码地理-气候关系，动态图编码近期相关性及台风影响；3）通过两个图神经网络提取目标节点表示，经空间前缀融合后注入LLM。

Result: 在南海海温预测任务中，该完整配置在十个预测步长内取得了最低的均方根误差（MAE）和最高的决定系数（R²），优于所有对比方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法不仅实现了高精度的数值预测，还通过规则模块匹配预测趋势与环境因子，提供了带有来源链接的事后上下文解释，增强了模型的可解释性。

Abstract: Sea surface temperature (SST) forecasting depends on local temporal persistence, regional spatial dependence, and environmental conditions that evolve with the forecast date. We study how these heterogeneous conditions can be presented to a large language model (LLM) for regional multi-step forecasting without serializing the full SST grid as text. We formulate forecasting as conditional numerical generation: historical SST and anomaly sequences, date-aligned environmental records, and static ocean knowledge form a textual context, while regional spatial state is supplied through continuous graph-derived prefixes. A static graph encodes persistent geographic--climatological relations, and a dynamic graph encodes recent SST correlations and localized tropical-cyclone influence. Two graph neural networks produce a target-node representation that is mapped by a spatial-prefix fusion and injected into the LLM input. On SST forecasting in the South China Sea, the complete configuration achieves the best MAE and $\Rtwo$ among the compared methods over ten forecast steps. Alongside the numerical forecast, a rule-based module matches predicted trends and environmental-factor directions with knowledge entries to return source-linked, post-hoc contextual explanations.

</details>


### [100] [Isotropic Yet Undecodable: The Sequential Content-Sufficiency Gap in Latent-Predictive Text Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07906)
*K. P. Santoso,N. Z. Fadil,F. P. Harsanti,R. V. H. Ginardi,G. N. Iyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究序列内容的充分性，提出CANOPE框架。实验表明，仅靠几何正则化（各向同性高斯分布）不足以保证序列信息的可恢复性，位置感知监督显著优于无监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究表示是否保留了输入中有序的靶标信息，分析确定性规范锚点施加的限制，并解释为何仅凭秩无法决定预测风险。

Method: 1. 采用信息论分解方法分离输入歧义、表示损失和读取不匹配；2. 构建可恢复视角以揭示零靶标信息与完美一致性及各向同性高斯性的共存现象；3. 提出CANOPE框架，包含有序潜在画布、规范Token监督和几何正则化；4. 使用Token对数损失作为单侧信息损失界限。

Result: 在40,000个验证序列上，PL2的Positional Recall@1（98.8%）远高于PL0（13.5%）；在LJSpeech语音合成任务中，结合冻结PL2的MatchaTTS字错误率（WER）为21.54%，显著低于冻结PL0的99.22%。

Conclusion: 几何正则性本身不能保证可恢复的序列内容或在文本设置下的有效下游访问，位置特定的监督对于保留有序信息至关重要。

Abstract: We study sequential content sufficiency by investigating whether a representation retains the ordered target information available in its input. An information-theoretic decomposition separates input ambiguity, representation loss, and readout mismatch. We construct recoverable views where perfect agreement and joint isotropic Gaussianity coexist with zero target information, and establish limits imposed by deterministic canonical anchors. Token log-loss provides a one-sided information-loss bound; a fixed-penalty ridge analysis shows why rank alone cannot determine prediction risk. These results motivate CANOPE, a nonautoregressive framework with ordered latent canvases, canonical-token supervision, and geometric regularization. On 40,000 validation sequences, latent-agreement (PL0) and token-grounded (PL2) have nearly identical pooled ranks but reach 13.5% and 98.8% positional Recall@1, respectively, under strong natural corruption when the correct target length is provided. On 3,930 LJSpeech validation utterances, frozen PL2 with a trained MatchaTTS readout yields 21.54% word error rate (WER) on corrupted text, versus 99.22% for frozen PL0, while end-to-end MatchaTTS reaches 10.93%. These results show that geometric regularity alone does not guarantee recoverable sequential content or effective downstream access in the text settings studied here.

</details>


### [101] [Continuous Memory Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07907)
*Ciaran Regan,Kai Arulkumaran,Luke Darlow,Stefania Druga,Sebastian Risi,Llion Jones*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Continuous Memory Machine (CMM)，一种具有矩阵值短/长期记忆状态的循环架构，通过Transformer联合更新实现双向读写，在多项任务上优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统RNN中短期计算与长期保留共享同一表示导致的瓶颈，弥补现有扩展方案缺乏生物样式的快速神经元级处理与长期保持能力结合的问题。

Method: 构建CMM架构，利用CTM基础，设独立参数化神经元模型用于短期记忆处理近期活动模式；设持久化长期记忆存储信息；使用Transformer联合更新双记忆库，实现表达力强且可双向读写的机制（互相重组内容及交叉读写）。
